Chinese Vice President Han Zheng will travel to New York from September 24 to 26 to participate in the general debate of the 81st session of the United Nations General Assembly, the Foreign Ministry spokesperson announced on September 22. During the visit, he will also attend the high-level dialogue on the fifth anniversary of the Global Development Initiative, hosted by China, and hold meetings with the UN Secretary-General, the President of the 81st UNGA, and leaders of relevant countries. The announcement was published by Xinhua News Agency on September 22 and appeared in the People&\#x27;s Daily on September 23, 2026.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 23, 00:26

**「Background」** The United Nations General Assembly holds an annual general debate where heads of state and government address the organization on global issues. China&\#x27;s Vice President has represented the country at previous UNGA sessions, often highlighting China&\#x27;s development cooperation and multilateral engagement priorities.

**「Impact」** The attendance signals China&\#x27;s continued active participation in UN multilateral processes and its emphasis on the Global Development Initiative as a diplomatic priority.

<a id="item-tech-news-177"></a>
### [China plans four-pronged strategy to boost inbound tourism retail](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/23/content_30182664.html) ⭐️ ?/10

A September 23, 2026 People&\#x27;s Daily editorial argues that China should leverage its super-large market to upgrade inbound consumption through four operational strategies: adding high-quality supply, subtracting institutional costs, multiplying format integration, and dividing circulation barriers. The article highlights that in 2025, duty-free sales for departing visitors nearly doubled year-on-year, with five key international consumption centers—Shanghai, Beijing, Guangzhou, Tianjin, and Chongqing—accounting for one-third of foreign arrivals and two-thirds of退税 sales. The authors, affiliated with Zhejiang Gongshang University, propose measures such as nationwide互认 for immediate duty refunds, digital tax-free services, cross-sector tourism-retail integration, and removing barriers for domestically produced goods to compete globally.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 23, 00:26

**「Background」** China has been expanding visa-free entry and payment convenience for foreigners to attract inbound tourism and consumption. Duty-free shopping for departing international visitors has become a significant policy tool, with &\#x27;China travel&\#x27; and &\#x27;China shopping&\#x27; emerging as trends among global visitors.

**「Impact」** The proposed measures could significantly increase spending by international visitors in China&\#x27;s major cities and strengthen the competitiveness of Chinese retail and service sectors. However, full implementation depends on inter-agency coordination and infrastructure upgrades across multiple municipalities.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-178"></a>
### [Rail-tourism integration boosts cultural consumption in China](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/23/content_30182665.html) ⭐️ ?/10

In September 2026, the People&\#x27;s Daily reported on the growing trend of using tourist trains as immersive cultural and tourism experiences rather than mere transportation. During the summer travel rush from July 1 to August 31, 2026, China operated 699 tourist trains, including 415 tourist-specific trains and 284 tourist lines. The article highlights three examples: the G3998 &quot;Long March High-Speed Rail&quot; connecting Ruijin and Yan&\#x27;an for revolutionary heritage tourism; the C5762 Sichuan-Qinghai tourist line featuring Qiang and Tibetan cultural performances and intangible cultural heritage displays; and the &quot;Qilu No.1&quot; senior-focused tourist train in Shandong with accessibility features like anti-slip mats and elderly-friendly amenities. These initiatives align with China&\#x27;s 15th Five-Year Plan for Building a Strong Tourism Country, which promotes railway-tourism integration. Since its April 2025 launch, the Qilu No.1 train has carried over 1.27 million passengers.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 23, 00:26

**「Background」** China has been developing specialized tourist trains that combine transportation with cultural tourism experiences, integrating features like themed decorations, local cuisine, and on-board entertainment. The 15th Five-Year Plan period \(2026-2030\) includes specific planning for strengthening tourism infrastructure and promoting cultural-railway integration.

**「Impact」** Tourist trains are creating new consumption scenarios that boost regional tourism economies, particularly benefiting revolutionary base areas and ethnic minority regions through improved accessibility and themed experiences.

---

<a id="item-tech-news-179"></a>
### [China warns consumers against deceptive water purifier scams targeting elderly](http://paper.people.com.cn/rmrb/pc/content/202609/23/content_30182666.html) ⭐️ ?/10

On September 23, 2026, China&\#x27;s consumer protection authorities highlighted a surge in deceptive marketing of water purifiers aimed at elderly consumers in rural and community settings. Complaints via the national 315 platform exceeded the total for all of 2024 in the first seven months alone, with July seeing a 40.17% increase over January. The China Consumers Association issued five key warnings: avoid high-pressure sales events offering free gifts, verify products carry valid CCC safety marks and health permits, assess actual filtration performance and filter replacement costs, insist on official invoices and warranty documentation, and preserve evidence to pursue legal remedies including triple-damages compensation for fraud under the Consumer Rights Protection Law.

rss · 人民日报\(People&\#x27;s Daily\) · Sep 23, 00:26

**「Background」** Water purifiers sold through mobile or temporary vendors often target older adults with exaggerated health claims that contradict the devices&\#x27; actual function of improving water quality. Mandatory certification systems in China, such as the CCC mark for electrical appliances and health permits issued by provincial health authorities, exist to ensure product safety and compliance.

**「Impact」** Elderly consumers and rural households should now expect stricter scrutiny of water purifier sales tactics and are advised to purchase only through licensed retailers with complete documentation. Vulnerable buyers face higher risks of financial loss and difficulty enforcing warranties when transactions occur via personal WeChat payments without receipts.

---

### [Trump praises Burnham as a natural businessperson](https://www.theguardian.com/politics/2026/sep/22/donald-trump-praises-natual-businessman-andy-burnham-after-first-meeting) ⭐️ ?/10

US President Donald Trump has praised British Prime Minister Andy Burnham as a &quot;natural businessperson&quot; following their first face-to-face meeting at the United Nations in New York. Trump told reporters that he and Burnham discussed a range of topics including Rolls-Royce, energy, and immigration, and suggested the UK was &quot;more up&quot; under Burnham than it was under former Prime Minister Keir Starmer. However, Trump also criticized the UK&\#x27;s decision to cede the Chagos Islands, berating the move during the same encounter.

rss · 观察家报\(The Observer\) · Sep 22, 18:09

**「Background」** Andy Burnham is a British Labour Party politician who currently serves as Prime Minister of the United Kingdom, having succeeded Keir Starmer. The meeting between Trump and Burnham took place at the UN General Assembly in New York, where world leaders gather annually for high-level diplomatic discussions.

**「Impact」** The exchange highlights the evolving dynamics of US-UK relations under Trump&\#x27;s second presidency, with personal rapport between leaders potentially influencing cooperation on trade and security issues. Trump&\#x27;s criticism of the Chagos Islands decision suggests territorial sovereignty disputes remain a point of friction in bilateral relations.

<a id="item-tech-news-356"></a>
### [DMZ Explosion Injures Three Soldiers During Minefield Survey](https://world.kbs.co.kr/service/news_view.htm?lang=e&amp;Seq_Code=204433) ⭐️ ?/10

The Joint Chiefs of Staff confirmed that an explosion occurred on Monday in the heavily fortified Demilitarized Zone \(DMZ\), injuring three South Korean soldiers. The incident took place while military personnel were conducting an operation to identify a suspected minefield that North Korea may have laid south of the inter-Korean border. The military was actively surveying the area for landmines when the explosion happened.

rss · KBS\(Korean Broadcasting System\) · Sep 22, 08:43

**「Background」** The Demilitarized Zone \(DMZ\) separating North and South Korea is one of the most heavily fortified borders in the world, with extensive minefields on both sides. These mines, many laid during or after the Korean War, continue to pose significant risks to military operations and patrols in the area.

**「Impact」** Three South Korean soldiers were injured in the explosion, highlighting the ongoing dangers of mine-related incidents in the DMZ. This event underscores the continued risk to military personnel conducting surveys and operations near the inter-Korean border.

<a id="item-tech-news-372"></a>
### [Pope Leo XIV&\#x27;s peaceful Europe visit to Metz](https://www.lemonde.fr/societe/article/2026/09/22/a-metz-la-visite-du-pape-sous-le-sceau-de-l-europe-et-de-la-paix-sait-il-qu-il-va-servir-la-messe-sur-un-autel-fait-avec-le-bronze-d-anciens-canons-allemands_6780277_3224.html) ⭐️ ?/10

Pope Leo XIV is set to visit Metz on September 22, 2026, with a focus on multilateralism, disarmament, and European peace. He will deliver a speech on Europe at the Robert-Schuman center, accompanied by French President Emmanuel Macron and other yet-to-be-announced heads of state. The visit carries symbolic weight due to the local altar constructed from the bronze of former German cannons, underscoring the theme of reconciliation.

rss · 世界报\(Le Monde\) · Sep 22, 17:00

**「Background」** Metz, located in the historically contested region of Lorraine, has seen multiple shifts in sovereignty between France and Germany. The Robert-Schuman center is named after the French statesman who proposed the European Coal and Steel Community, a foundational step toward European integration. The altar made from repurposed German cannons serves as a tangible symbol of post-war reconciliation.

**「Impact」** The papal visit reinforces the symbolic link between the Catholic Church and European political integration, particularly in a region emblematic of Franco-German reconciliation. It may strengthen public support for EU unity and disarmament initiatives, though the specific policy impacts remain uncertain without further details on the Pope&\#x27;s speech.

<a id="item-tech-news-392"></a>
### [Guatemala receives first US arms in nearly 50 years](https://www.france24.com/en/video/20260922-guatemala-receives-first-us-arms-in-nearly-50-years) ⭐️ ?/10

Guatemala received its first batch of military weapons from the United States in nearly five decades, marking the end of a long-standing US arms embargo. The embargo had been in place due to human rights violations committed during Guatemala&\#x27;s civil war. Despite the historical restrictions, the US has continued to cooperate with Guatemala in recent years on counter-drug trafficking efforts.

rss · France 24 · Sep 22, 14:05

**「Background」** The United States imposed an arms embargo on Guatemala during and after its civil war \(1960-1996\) due to widespread human rights violations, including atrocities committed by government forces. This embargo persisted for decades as a condition of US foreign policy regarding human rights in Central America.

**「Impact」** This shipment represents a significant shift in US-Guatemala military relations, potentially enabling Guatemala to enhance its defense capabilities beyond drug interdiction cooperation. The decision reflects evolving security priorities and the normalization of bilateral military ties after decades of restriction.

<a id="item-tech-news-476"></a>
### [Puerto Viejo de Talamanca: A Relaxed Caribbean Coast Town](https://telegram.me/nature/20328) ⭐️ ?/10

Puerto Viejo de Talamanca is a small coastal town located on the Caribbean coast of Costa Rica. It is widely recognized as one of the most atmospheric and relaxed destinations within the country, offering a distinct cultural and scenic contrast to Costa Rica&\#x27;s Pacific coastline.

telegram · nature · Sep 22, 17:30

**「Background」** Costa Rica features two distinct coastlines: the Pacific Ocean to the west and the Caribbean Sea to the east. Puerto Viejo, situated in the Limón Province, reflects Afro-Caribbean influences in its cuisine, music, and culture, differentiating it from the more commercially developed Pacific beach towns.

**「Impact」** As a tourist destination, Puerto Viejo provides travelers with an alternative laid-back Caribbean experience within Costa Rica. Its reputation for relaxation and atmosphere continues to attract visitors seeking non-commercialized coastal environments.

<a id="item-tech-news-478"></a>
### [Everywhere cameras may make 2D photos obsolete](https://telegram.me/science/4384) ⭐️ ?/10

A Telegram science post argues that, in principle, sufficiently dense networks of high-resolution overlapping cameras could replace ordinary photographs by reconstructing navigable 3D scene representations from multiple viewpoints. The idea involves estimating position, shape, depth, texture, and appearance so that virtual cameras can generate novel images from almost any angle, with AI inferring truly occluded regions rather than recording them. The discussion is tagged with Gaussian splatting and 3D reconstruction concepts, but the analysis of the item failed, leaving the claim at a conceptual level without technical validation or implementation details.

telegram · science · Sep 22, 01:48

**「Background」** Gaussian splatting and multi-view 3D reconstruction are computer vision techniques that recover geometry and appearance from multiple images, enabling novel view synthesis. The post refers to creating navigable 3D scenes from camera networks rather than storing static 2D photographs.

**「Impact」** If realized, this approach could change how visual moments are recorded and revisited, but the post provides no evidence, systems, timelines, or performance data to confirm practical impact for users, developers, or the photography ecosystem. Until such infrastructure exists, ordinary photographs remain the standard recording medium.
